Ruling: model error; i1040gi pp. 17-19, Steps 1 and 4. executable-triage-v2 (`reviews/2025/transcription/triage/dependent.json`; 1,446 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 3,102 partial records; 9,442 comparisons): 9,015 of 9,442 agree; 141 candidate-unknown, 126 project-unknown, 111 project-refuses, 40 value, 9 candidate-refuses; on returns the full interview completes: 23 candidate-unknown, 2 candidate-refuses. Every completed-return difference was enumerated with the witness cap lifted (triage.triage with max_witnesses unbounded, written to scratch, the recorded triage file untouched; causes assigned by a scratch classifier, none unassigned): (a) 2 candidate-refuses: the reply declares dependent.multiple_support on its dependent.claimed for every person, so it refuses classm.s3.4 and classm.s3.4.deepseek dep_a, a qualifying child, where i1040gi p. 19 applies multiple support only to the qualifying relative (Step 4, "For whom you provided over half of the person's support in 2025. But see ... Multiple support agreements"; p. 20, Multiple support agreements, "Sometimes no one provides more than half of the support of a person"); (b) 2 candidate-unknown: the reply asks dependent.lawful of a household_member (classm.clcw26.2 and .deepseek dep_b, a fixture that predates that question), whose relationship answer already carries the local-law condition (i1040gi p. 19, Step 4, "Any other person (other than your spouse) who lived with you all year as a member of your household if your relationship didn't violate local law"): an answer the source does not need; (c) 21 candidate-unknown partial records withholding an answer the reply reads first (17 multiple_support, 2 married, 2 divorced_rule). No completed value difference. The qualifying-child and qualifying-relative tests are otherwise the project's, including the kidnapped gate (the reply's own h_kidnap_issue is kidnapped and fewer than 7 months, as dependent.kidnap_residency) and the non-taxpayer test of Step 4 (dependent.other_nontaxpayer). Off completed returns the one reading difference of this round: the reply lifts the tie-breaker refusal when every other claimant is not a taxpayer, even for your own qualifying child, so it computes f1.t153.own_child_nontaxpayer_other (your child aged 3, the other person not a taxpayer; candidate child tax credit $2,200.00) where the project refuses at dependent.qc_tiebreak. p501 pp. 15 and 17, Tiebreaker rules, have no non-taxpayer exception and still compare the parents who can claim the child (p. 17, "the child is treated as the qualifying child of the person who had the highest AGI for the year, but only if that person’s AGI is higher than the highest AGI of any of the child’s parents who can claim the child"); the non-taxpayer sentence is stated for the qualifying-relative test only (i1040gi p. 19, Step 4, "For this purpose, a person isn't a taxpayer if ..."). The project's refusal is a cited screen; scope, and listed as an owner question in the F1 report; f1.t153.friend_child_taxpayer is refused by both. Project stands.

Off completed returns the one reading difference of this round: the reply lifts the tie-breaker refusal when every other claimant is not a taxpayer, even for your own qualifying child, so it computes f1.t153.own_child_nontaxpayer_other (your child aged 3, the other person not a taxpayer; candidate child tax credit $2,200.00) where the project refuses at dependent.qc_tiebreak. p501 pp. 15 and 17, Tiebreaker rules, have no non-taxpayer exception and still compare the parents who can claim the child (p. 17, "the child is treated as the qualifying child of the person who had the highest AGI for the year, but only if that person’s AGI is higher than the highest AGI of any of the child’s parents who can claim the child"); the non-taxpayer sentence is stated for the qualifying-relative test only (i1040gi p. 19, Step 4, "For this purpose, a person isn't a taxpayer if ..."). Every completed-return difference was enumerated with the witness cap lifted (triage.triage with max_witnesses unbounded, written to scratch, the recorded triage file untouched; causes assigned by a scratch classifier, none unassigned): (a) 2 candidate-refuses: the reply declares dependent.multiple_support on its dependent.claimed for every person, so it refuses classm.s3.4 and classm.s3.4.deepseek dep_a, a qualifying child, where i1040gi p. 19 applies multiple support only to the qualifying relative (Step 4, "For whom you provided over half of the person's support in 2025. But see ... Multiple support agreements"; p. 20, Multiple support agreements, "Sometimes no one provides more than half of the support of a person"); (b) 14 candidate-unknown partial records withholding multiple_support, which the reply reads for every person. No completed value difference. The amount is the project's choose on dependent.ctc_child and inherits it. Off completed returns the one reading difference of this round: the reply lifts the tie-breaker refusal when every other claimant is not a taxpayer, even for your own qualifying child, so it computes f1.t153.own_child_nontaxpayer_other (your child aged 3, the other person not a taxpayer; candidate child tax credit $2,200.00) where the project refuses at dependent.qc_tiebreak. p501 pp. 15 and 17, Tiebreaker rules, have no non-taxpayer exception and still compare the parents who can claim the child (p. 17, "the child is treated as the qualifying child of the person who had the highest AGI for the year, but only if that person’s AGI is higher than the highest AGI of any of the child’s parents who can claim the child"); the non-taxpayer sentence is stated for the qualifying-relative test only (i1040gi p. 19, Step 4, "For this purpose, a person isn't a taxpayer if ..."). The project's refusal is a cited screen; scope, and listed as an owner question in the F1 report. Project stands.

Every completed-return difference was enumerated with the witness cap lifted (triage.triage with max_witnesses unbounded, written to scratch, the recorded triage file untouched; causes assigned by a scratch classifier, none unassigned): (a) 2 candidate-refuses: the reply declares dependent.multiple_support on its dependent.claimed for every person, so it refuses classm.s3.4 and classm.s3.4.deepseek dep_a, a qualifying child, where i1040gi p. 19 applies multiple support only to the qualifying relative (Step 4, "For whom you provided over half of the person's support in 2025. But see ... Multiple support agreements"; p. 20, Multiple support agreements, "Sometimes no one provides more than half of the support of a person"); (b) 14 candidate-unknown partial records withholding multiple_support, which the reply reads for every person. No completed value difference. The amount is the project's choose on dependent.ctc_child and inherits it (own_child_nontaxpayer_other: candidate $1,700.00). Off completed returns the one reading difference of this round: the reply lifts the tie-breaker refusal when every other claimant is not a taxpayer, even for your own qualifying child, so it computes f1.t153.own_child_nontaxpayer_other (your child aged 3, the other person not a taxpayer; candidate child tax credit $2,200.00) where the project refuses at dependent.qc_tiebreak. p501 pp. 15 and 17, Tiebreaker rules, have no non-taxpayer exception and still compare the parents who can claim the child (p. 17, "the child is treated as the qualifying child of the person who had the highest AGI for the year, but only if that person’s AGI is higher than the highest AGI of any of the child’s parents who can claim the child"); the non-taxpayer sentence is stated for the qualifying-relative test only (i1040gi p. 19, Step 4, "For this purpose, a person isn't a taxpayer if ..."). Every completed-return difference was enumerated with the witness cap lifted (triage.triage with max_witnesses unbounded, written to scratch, the recorded triage file untouched; causes assigned by a scratch classifier, none unassigned): (a) 2 candidate-refuses: the reply declares dependent.multiple_support on its dependent.claimed for every person, so it refuses classm.s3.4 and classm.s3.4.deepseek dep_a, a qualifying child, where i1040gi p. 19 applies multiple support only to the qualifying relative (Step 4, "For whom you provided over half of the person's support in 2025. But see ... Multiple support agreements"; p. 20, Multiple support agreements, "Sometimes no one provides more than half of the support of a person"); (b) 2 candidate-unknown: the reply asks dependent.lawful of a household_member (classm.clcw26.2 and .deepseek dep_b, a fixture that predates that question), whose relationship answer already carries the local-law condition (i1040gi p. 19, Step 4, "Any other person (other than your spouse) who lived with you all year as a member of your household if your relationship didn't violate local law"): an answer the source does not need; (c) 41 project-unknown partial records withholding dependent.absence_no_return, which the project's absence test reads (hoh_absence, T-091) and the interview asks; (d) 2 project-unknown partial records withholding dependent.other_claimant, which the refusal gate dependent.qc_tiebreak reads; (e) 18 candidate-unknown partial records (17 multiple_support, 1 spouse_relative). Second transcription's note: The box refers to the time the care was provided. A person 14 or older at the end of 2025 was over 12 throughout the year. For age 13, the over-12 care question decides. With no care expenses there is no care to report, so the box is left unchecked.

Ruling: model error; i2441 p. 3, Column (c): "Check the box in column (c) if the qualifying person listed in column (a) was over age 12 at the time the care was provided and was disabled". executable-triage-v2 (`reviews/2025/transcription/triage/dependent.json`; 1,446 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 3,102 partial records; 9,442 comparisons): 9,048 of 9,442 agree; 243 candidate-unknown, 83 candidate-refuses, 66 project-unknown, 2 project-refuses; on returns the full interview completes: 2 candidate-refuses. Every completed-return difference was enumerated with the witness cap lifted (triage.triage with max_witnesses unbounded, written to scratch, the recorded triage file untouched; causes assigned by a scratch classifier, none unassigned): 2 candidate-refuses: the reply declares dependent.multiple_support on its dependent.claimed for every person, so it refuses classm.s3.4 and classm.s3.4.deepseek dep_a, a qualifying child, where i1040gi p. 19 applies multiple support only to the qualifying relative (Step 4, "For whom you provided over half of the person's support in 2025. But see ... Multiple support agreements"; p. 20, Multiple support agreements, "Sometimes no one provides more than half of the support of a person"). The box test is otherwise the project's. Off completed returns the reply asks dc_incapable where the project hides the line (for example f1.t113.dep_llc_applied, a student with no care). Project stands.

Ruling: model error; f8863 p. 2, line 31; i8863 p. 3, Who cannot claim a credit, "5. The student has not been issued a TIN by the due date of his or her 2025 return (including extensions)." executable-triage-v2 (`reviews/2025/transcription/triage/dependent.json`; 1,446 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 3,102 partial records; 9,442 comparisons): 6,200 of 9,442 agree; 3,161 candidate-unknown, 77 candidate-refuses, 3 project-refuses, 1 value; on returns the full interview completes: 1,077 candidate-unknown, 2 candidate-refuses. Every completed-return difference was enumerated with the witness cap lifted (triage.triage with max_witnesses unbounded, written to scratch, the recorded triage file untouched; causes assigned by a scratch classifier, none unassigned): (a) 1,065 candidate-unknown dependent.edu on returns with f8863.paid no, where the project hides the line and does not ask it (69 fixtures and 996 partial records; for example classm.f4952.1.deepseek dep_a): the reply has no f8863.paid test; (b) 4 candidate-unknown dependent.edu_tin_issued on American opportunity credit students (classm.s3.2, classm.f8863.2 and their .deepseek copies): the reply requires an issued TIN for the American opportunity credit, which p970 p. 11 does not ("If an ATIN or ITIN is applied for on or before the due date of a 2025 return (including extensions) and the IRS issues an ATIN or ITIN as a result of the application, the IRS will consider the ATIN or ITIN as is- sued on or before the due date of the return."; owner decision T-113 keeps that deeming); off completed returns the same reading gives f1.t113.dep_aoc_applied (stage19.example.jack_kay with the ATIN applied for) line 27 $0.00 and line 30 $0.00 where the project gives $4,000.00 and $2,500.00; (c) 6 candidate-unknown partial records withholding multiple_support; (d) 2 candidate-unknown: the reply asks dependent.lawful of a household_member (classm.clcw26.2 and .deepseek dep_b, a fixture that predates that question), whose relationship answer already carries the local-law condition (i1040gi p. 19, Step 4, "Any other person (other than your spouse) who lived with you all year as a member of your household if your relationship didn't violate local law"): an answer the source does not need; (e) 2 candidate-refuses: the reply declares dependent.multiple_support on its dependent.claimed for every person, so it refuses classm.s3.4 and classm.s3.4.deepseek dep_a, a qualifying child, where i1040gi p. 19 applies multiple support only to the qualifying relative (Step 4, "For whom you provided over half of the person's support in 2025. But see ... Multiple support agreements"; p. 20, Multiple support agreements, "Sometimes no one provides more than half of the support of a person"). Off completed returns, the T-113 case: f1.t113.dep_llc_applied (a lifetime learning credit student whose ATIN was only applied for) is refused by the project at dependent.edu_llc_tin_refused, as the owner decided (T-113: refuse the lifetime learning credit for a student with only an applied-for ATIN or ITIN), and the reply computes line 31 $0.00 by excluding the student; the difference is a refusal on a cited gate (scope). Project stands.

## Raw second transcription

```yaml
```yaml
lines:
  - id: dependent.one
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "One (this person, counted once)"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: param, ref: dependent.one}

  - id: dependent.h_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Zero"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}

  - id: dependent.h_one_cent
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "One cent (flag value)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sc.one_cent}

  # ---------- Step 1: qualifying child ----------
  - id: dependent.h_rel_qc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Son, daughter, stepchild, foster child, brother, sister, stepbrother, stepsister, half brother, half sister, or a descendant of any of them"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "child"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling_descendant"}]}

  - id: dependent.h_age_qc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Under age 19 and younger than you (or spouse if filing jointly); or under age 24, a full-time student, and younger; or any age and permanently and totally disabled"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {param: qc.age_limit}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.younger, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {param: qc.student_age_limit}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.student, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.younger, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.disabled, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_qc_core
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Meets the conditions to be your qualifying child (relationship, age, support, joint return and residency tests)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_rel_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_age_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.self_support, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.joint_return, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, ge: [dependent.months, {const: 7, type: Count}]}
    note: "The months input already applies the exceptions to time lived with you (temporary absences, birth, death, adoption or placement), so residency is months of 7 or more."

  - id: dependent.h_tiebreak
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualifying child of more than one person (tiebreaker rules apply)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_core, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.other_claimant, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.other_nontaxpayer, {const: false}]}
    note: "The tiebreaker rules depend on parentage, time lived with each person and AGI, which are not in the interface. The tool refuses when another taxpayer could also treat the child as a qualifying child. If every such other person is not a taxpayer, it treats the child as yours."

  - id: dependent.h_kidnap_issue
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Kidnapped child who fails the time-lived-with-you test as counted"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.kidnapped, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, lt: [dependent.months, {const: 7, type: Count}]}
    note: "The kidnapped-child rule (Pub. 501) needs facts about the years of the kidnapping and the return, which are not supplied. The tool refuses only when that rule would matter."

  - id: dependent.h_citizen_cm
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "U.S. citizen, U.S. national, U.S. resident alien, or a resident of Canada or Mexico"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.citizen, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.canada_mexico, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_filer_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Filing a joint return, or you could not be claimed as a dependent on someone else's 2025 return"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [sa.dependent, {const: false}]}

  - id: dependent.h_claimed_qc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "You can claim this qualifying child as a dependent (Step 2)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_core, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_citizen_cm, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_filer_ok, {const: true}]}

  # ---------- Step 4: qualifying relative ----------
  - id: dependent.h_rel_direct
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Related to you (or, on a joint return, to your spouse) in a way that need not live with you all year"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "child"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "parent"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "parent_relative"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "in_law"}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.spouse_relative, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_rel_qr
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Member of household or relationship test"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_rel_direct, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.qr_all_year, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.lawful, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_not_qc_any
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Wasn't a qualifying child of any taxpayer for 2025"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_core, {const: false}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.other_claimant, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.other_nontaxpayer, {const: true}]}
    note: "The other_claimant input also covers the EIC rules, which have no support test. The tool uses it as given."

  - id: dependent.h_gi_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Had gross income of less than $5,200 in 2025"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, lt: [dependent.gross_income, {param: qr.gross_income_limit}]}
    note: "The sheltered-workshop exception to the gross income test is not computed. Gross income is taken as entered."

  - id: dependent.h_claimed_qr
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "You can claim this qualifying relative as a dependent (Step 4)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_rel_qr, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_not_qc_any, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_gi_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.supported, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_citizen_cm, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_filer_ok, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.claimed
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "You can claim this person as a dependent"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_claimed_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_claimed_qr, {const: true}]}
    refuses: [dependent.married, dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.multiple_support, dependent.h_tiebreak, dependent.h_kidnap_issue]

  # ---------- Steps 3 and 5: CTC / ODC ----------
  - id: dependent.ctc_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualifying child for the child tax credit (Step 3)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_claimed_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.tin, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.citizen, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {param: ctc.age_limit}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [household.ctc_ssn, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.work_ssn, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.odc_person
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Dependent who qualifies you for the credit for other dependents"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [household.all_tin, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.tin, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.citizen, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_ctc_per
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$2,200 per qualifying child (Schedule 8812, line 5)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ctc.per_child}

  - id: dependent.ctc_amount
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Child tax credit amount for this child before phase-out"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.h_ctc_per}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: false}]}, then: dependent.h_zero}

  - id: dependent.h_odc_per
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$500 per other dependent (Schedule 8812, line 7)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: odc.per_dependent}

  - id: dependent.odc_amount
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Credit for other dependents amount for this person before phase-out"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.odc_person, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.h_odc_per}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.odc_person, {const: false}]}, then: dependent.h_zero}

  - id: dependent.h_actc_per
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$1,700 per qualifying child (Schedule 8812, line 16b)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: actc.per_child}

  - id: dependent.actc_amount
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Additional child tax credit amount for this child (line 16b)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.h_actc_per}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: false}]}, then: dependent.h_zero}

  # ---------- Head of household ----------
  - id: dependent.h_half
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Lived with you for more than half of 2025 (or of the part of the year alive or since adoption or placement)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, ge: [dependent.months, {const: 7, type: Count}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - op: test
              any:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.born_died, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.placed, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.part_year_home, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.hoh_test1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Test 1: your parent whom you can claim as a dependent, whose main home for all of 2025 you paid over half the cost of keeping up"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_claimed_qr, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "parent"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.parent_home_paid, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_household_only
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Your dependent only because the person lived with you for all of 2025"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_claimed_qr, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_rel_direct, {const: false}]}

  - id: dependent.hoh_test2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Test 2: a person who qualifies you for head of household and lived with you for more than half of the year"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_half, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_household_only, {const: false}]}
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_core, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: false}]}
    note: "Items 3 and 4 (a married qualifying child, a child under the divorced-parent rule) and multiple support agreements are refused through the screening inputs. Paying over half the cost of keeping up the home is a household test and is not part of this per-person result."

  - id: dependent.hoh_absence
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Person relied on for Test 2 whose time lived with you counts an absence from which return was not reasonably expected, or during which the home was not kept up"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.hoh_test2, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.absence_no_return, {const: true}]}

  # ---------- EIC ----------
  - id: dependent.h_eic_core
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Meets the qualifying child tests for the EIC (relationship, age, joint return, lived with you in the United States more than half of 2025)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_rel_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_age_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.joint_return, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.us_half, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_tiebreak_eic
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC qualifying child of more than one person"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_eic_core, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.other_claimant, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.other_nontaxpayer, {const: false}]}

  - id: dependent.h_kidnap_eic
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Kidnapped child who fails the United States residency test as counted"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.kidnapped, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.us_half, {const: false}]}

  - id: dependent.eic_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualifying child for the EIC"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_eic_core, {const: true}]}
    refuses: [dependent.married, dependent.h_tiebreak_eic, dependent.h_kidnap_eic]

  - id: dependent.eic_ssn_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualifying child for the EIC who has a valid SSN"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_child, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_ssn, {const: true}]}

  # ---------- Form 2441 ----------
  - id: dependent.h_dc_under13
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Under age 13 for some part of 2025 when care was paid for"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {param: dc.child_age}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.age, {param: dc.child_age}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_under13, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.dc_qp
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualifying person for the child and dependent care credit"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_claimed_qc, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_dc_under13, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_incapable, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_half, {const: true}]}
    note: "A disabled person who would be your dependent except for gross income, a joint return, or your being claimable, and the custodial-parent rule, are handled by the Form 2441 gates and screening inputs. This result covers only persons you can claim."

  - id: dependent.h_dc_flag
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "One cent if a qualifying person, otherwise zero"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.h_one_cent}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: false}]}, then: dependent.h_zero}

  - id: dependent.dc_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of qualifying persons this entry contributes (1 or 0)"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: percent_floor, part: dependent.h_dc_flag, whole: {param: sc.hundred_cents}, cap: 1}

  - id: dependent.dc_line2d
    printed: "2(d)"
    label: "Qualified expenses you incurred and paid in 2025 for the person listed in column (a)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.dc_paid}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: false}]}, then: dependent.h_zero}

  - id: dependent.dc_line2c
    printed: "2(c)"
    label: "Check here if the qualifying person was over age 12 and was disabled"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_incapable, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, gt: [dependent.dc_paid, {const: 0}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, ge: [dependent.age, {const: 14, type: Count}]}
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.age, {param: dc.child_age}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_over12_care, {const: true}]}
    note: "The box refers to the time the care was provided. A person 14 or older at the end of 2025 was over 12 throughout the year. For age 13, the over-12 care question decides. With no care expenses there is no care to report, so the box is left unchecked."

  # ---------- Form 8863 Part III ----------
  - id: dependent.h_edu_base
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Claimed dependent for whom qualified education expenses were paid"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_aoc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Student eligible for the American opportunity credit (lines 23-26 and the Form 1098-T, EIN and TIN requirements)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_base, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_1098t, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line23, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line24, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line25, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line26, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_ein, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_tin_issued, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_llc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Student for whom the lifetime learning credit is figured (line 31)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_base, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_aoc, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_1098t, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_tin_issued, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_edu_over_issue
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Assistance and refunds exceed an academic period's expenses (per-period allocation needed)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_over, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_edu_adj
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "AQEE Worksheet line 3: total adjustments (add lines 2a, 2b, and 2c)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [dependent.edu_a2025, dependent.edu_a2026, dependent.edu_refunds]}
    refuses: [dependent.h_edu_over_issue]

  - id: dependent.h_aoc_exp
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "AQEE Worksheet line 1 (American opportunity credit): tuition, required fees and course materials"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [dependent.edu_inst, dependent.edu_mat]}

  - id: dependent.h_aoc_adj
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "AQEE Worksheet line 4 (American opportunity credit). Subtract line 3 from line 1. If zero or less, enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: dependent.h_aoc_exp, take: dependent.h_edu_adj}

  - id: dependent.h_aoc_cap
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$4,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: aoc.expense_cap}

  - id: dependent.h_aoc_capped
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses, not more than $4,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [dependent.h_aoc_adj, dependent.h_aoc_cap]}

  - id: dependent.h_llc_adj
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "AQEE Worksheet line 4 (lifetime learning credit): expenses required to be paid to the institution less adjustments. If zero or less, enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: dependent.edu_inst, take: dependent.h_edu_adj}

  - id: dependent.h_band
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$2,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: aoc.full_band}

  - id: dependent.edu_line27
    printed: "27"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses (see instructions). Don't enter more than $4,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_aoc, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.h_aoc_capped}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_aoc, {const: false}]}, then: dependent.h_zero}

  - id: dependent.edu_line28
    printed: "28"
    label: "Subtract $2,000 from line 27. If zero or less, enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: dependent.edu_line27, take: dependent.h_band}

  - id: dependent.edu_line29
    printed: "29"
    label: "Multiply line 28 by 25% (0.25)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: dependent.edu_line28, rate: {param: aoc.second_rate}}

  - id: dependent.h_line29_plus
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Add $2,000 to the amount on line 29"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [dependent.edu_line29, dependent.h_band]}

  - id: dependent.edu_line30
    printed: "30"
    label: "If line 28 is zero, enter the amount from line 27. Otherwise, add $2,000 to the amount on line 29 and enter the result"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line28, {const: 0}]}, then: dependent.edu_line27}
        - {guard: {op: test, gt: [dependent.edu_line28, {const: 0}]}, then: dependent.h_line29_plus}

  - id: dependent.edu_line31
    printed: "31"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses (see instructions)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_llc, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.h_llc_adj}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_llc, {const: false}]}, then: dependent.h_zero}

  # ---------- Form 8962 Worksheet 1-2 ----------
  - id: dependent.h_ptc_counted
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Dependent required to file an income tax return because their income meets the filing threshold"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.ptc_files, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.ptc_magi_counted
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This dependent's modified AGI included on Worksheet 1-2 (Form 8962, line 2b)"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_ptc_counted, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.ptc_magi}
        - {guard: {op: test, not: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_ptc_counted, {const: true}]}}, then: dependent.h_zero}
```
```
